### Beyond LoRA vs. Full Fine-Tuning: Gradient-Guided Optimizer Routing for LLM Adaptation

问题与演进：固定 LoRA 在低秩更新足够时节省状态，FFT 在高秩知识更新时保留容量；MoLF 让全部路径接收完整梯度，只由 optimizer 依据预条件下降分数稀疏提交更新。论文证据限于三任务、三个小模型和单卡实验，不能证明通用最优路由、训练吞吐或大模型稳定性；静态 LoRA/FFT 与显存受限的 MoLF-E 仍是共存分支。

Method：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.07111v1 §4.1–§4.5 MoLF architecture、Sparse AdamW routing 与 fusion`。

Evaluation：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.07111v1 §5.1–§5.4；Appendix A–B（3 tasks、Gemma/Qwen、single H100 or RTX PRO 6000、routing ablations）`。

Non-proof / fallback：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.07111v1 §6（first-order EPD、single FFT/LoRA expert、MoLF-E capacity boundary）`。离开披露条件时回退既有机制，不把作者结果外推为通用保证。

Artifact：`exact-v1 未给出作者代码仓库；event-time commit Not Disclosed`。

### Region4Web: Rethinking Observation Space Granularity for Web Agents

问题与演进：Element-level AXTree 保留完整可操作对象但让页面功能结构和跨步变化隐式存在；Region4Web 先划分功能区域，PageDigest 再按任务选择区域并在同页增量维护 observation，选择不足时以 view_all 回退。收益与失败只在 WebArena 及披露 actor/evaluator 下成立，不能外推动态站点、隐藏 DOM 或生产浏览器安全。

Evaluation：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.07134v1 §5；Appendix C–G（WebArena 812 tasks、30-step cap、4 backbones、SteP/AgentOccam）`。

Non-proof / fallback：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.07134v1 Appendix A（AXTree/URL transition assumptions、selection and auxiliary-model errors）`。离开披露条件时回退既有机制，不把作者结果外推为通用保证。

### Learning Agent Routing From Early Experience

问题与演进：始终调用 Agent 提高能力但承担高延迟，直接 LLM 更快却可能失败；BoundaryRouter 用部署早期经验构造边界样例，再以 rubric reasoning 决定升级。它把 routing proposal 与 Agent execution 分开，但早期经验质量、冷启动与分布漂移仍可能误路由；证据只覆盖 RouteBench 的二元路由，不能证明多 Agent 或高风险执行的授权正确性。

Evaluation：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.07180v1 §4–§5；Appendix A（RouteBench in-domain、paraphrase、OOD 与 ablations）`。

Non-proof / fallback：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.07180v1 §6（binary LLM-versus-one-agent routing；multi-agent/tool routing未验证）`。离开披露条件时回退既有机制，不把作者结果外推为通用保证。

Artifact：`https://github.com/MiroMindAI/MiroFlow 为 exact-v1 链接的相关实现入口；paper-specific immutable commit Not Disclosed`。

#### VNN-LIB 2.0: Rigorous Foundations for Neural Network Verification

问题与演进：`VNN-LIB 2.0: Rigorous Foundations for Neural Network Verification` 通过“Building on this foundation, we present a formal syntax for a more expressive query language, a type system for it over the numeric domains provided by the network theory, and finally a formal semantics.”改变 platform evaluation system 的可观察机制或决策边界；exact-v1 的证明范围限于“VNN-LIB~2.0 therefore provides robust and rigorous foundations for trustworthy neural network verification.”，不能外推到未披露 workload、model、hardware、precision、evaluator 或 SLO。旧方案在其原 workload、风险和成本约束下仍成立。

Method：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.07451v1 §3 VNN-LIB 2.0 language and semantics — mechanism: Building on this foundation, we present a formal syntax for a more expressive query language, a type system for it over the numeric domains provided by the network theory, and finally a formal semantics.`。

<!-- review:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-07514:start -->
### Is the Future Compatible? Diagnosing Dynamic Consistency in World Action Models

问题与演进：只评价 predicted video 或 action success 会漏掉二者是否描述同一转移；论文用执行后的真实 observation 定义 action-state consistency，并以候选 future consensus 构造无需环境重置的选择 proxy。该信号可能被静态背景主导且不能纠正 world model，真实环境仍拥有状态真值和动作提交权；结果只绑定两套 WAM、两套机器人 benchmark 与披露硬件。

Method：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.07514v1 §2–§4（latent MSE consistency、environment-exploring upper bound、future-consensus selection）`。

Evaluation：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.07514v1 §5；Appendix C–G（Cosmos-Policy/RoboCasa、LingBot-VA/RoboTwin 2.0、N=8、8 RTX 5090）`。

Non-proof / fallback：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.07514v1 Appendix H（background collapse；selection不修复底层 dynamics）`。离开披露条件时回退既有机制，不把作者结果外推为通用保证。

Artifact：`exact-v1 仅链接 Cosmos-Policy 与 LingBot-VA 上游仓库；paper-specific implementation commit Not Disclosed`。
<!-- claim:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-07514:start -->长期结论只限 arXiv:2605.07514v1 披露的方法、实验与限制；未披露 model、hardware、precision、length、batch、concurrency、evaluator 或 SLO 记为 Not Disclosed。<!-- claim:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-07514:end -->

<!-- review:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-07547:start -->
### Deadline-Driven Hierarchical Agentic Resource Sharing for AI Services and RAN Functions in AI-RAN

问题与演进：AI service 与 RAN 各自独占资源时责任清楚，却在 edge GPU 稀缺时浪费可共享容量；分层框架先提出 placement，再由 critic 估计 deadline 风险，allocation layer 才分配 GPU/CPU。收益来自论文负载与 SLO 定义，代价是预测误差、控制开销和高负载失稳；deadline 证据不足时仍应保留 reservation、静态优先级或拒绝 admission。

Method：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.07547v1 §II–§III（placement candidate generator、predictive critic、deadline-aware GPU/CPU allocator）`。

Evaluation：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.07547v1 §IV（ablation、overall SLO fulfillment、load sensitivity）`。

Non-proof / fallback：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.07547v1 §V；exact-v1 未建立跨 edge topology、不同 RAN/AI workload 或 production fault 的保证`。离开披露条件时回退既有机制，不把作者结果外推为通用保证。

Artifact：`exact-v1 未披露代码仓库或 immutable commit`。

### Bridging Modalities, Spanning Time: Structured Memory for Ultra-Long Agentic Video Reasoning

问题与演进：把超长视频压成单一摘要成本低但会丢失跨模态实体与跨时间事件；MAGIC-Video 以 typed multimodal graph 保存局部关系，再以 narrative chains 汇总长期实体/活动供检索。收益伴随 caption、entity linking、图构建、链过度注入和 judge 成本；原视频仍是证据权威，检索失败或高风险判断应回退原片段。

<!-- review:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-08314:start -->
### FlashSVD v1.5: Making Low-Rank Transformers Inference Actually Fast

问题与演进：SVD checkpoint 减少参数却会把执行切成多个小算子，朴素 runtime 可能比 dense 更慢；FlashSVD 在 decode 以 per-layer graph replay、dense KV 与 packed MLP 合并重复路径。收益取决于 checkpoint family、retained ratio、生成长度和 backend，额外 graph/build/fidelity 验收仍有成本；不支持的模型或硬件应回退 dense/标准 runtime。

Method：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.08314v1 §3（unified runtime、dense-KV attention、packed MLP、per-layer graph replay）`。

<!-- review:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-08399:start -->
### CoCoDA: Co-evolving Compositional DAG for Tool-Augmented Agents

问题与演进：固定 tool library 易审计但组合能力受限，纯文本自增长又会失去类型与依赖；CoCoDA 让 planner 与 typed compositional code DAG 依据成功轨迹共同更新，并用多层 retrieval 限制搜索成本。收益依赖 teacher、surrogate reward 与任务 verifier，错误工具或错误 credit 会随 library growth 放大；高风险工具仍需独立 schema、授权和执行验证。

Method：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.08399v1 §3（typed DAG retrieval cascade、planner/library coupled update、surrogate reward）`。

Evaluation：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.08399v1 §4；Appendix B–K（math、tabular、code tasks、library growth、ablation、sensitivity）`。

Non-proof / fallback：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.08399v1 §3.4、§5；Appendix L（theoretical assumptions、teacher/verifier dependence、growing-library cost）`。离开披露条件时回退既有机制，不把作者结果外推为通用保证。

Artifact：`exact-v1 附录披露 prompts、hyperparameters 与 library snapshots，但未给出公开 repo/immutable commit`。

### A Semantic-Sampling Framework for Evaluating Calibration in Open-Ended Question Answering

问题与演进：开放式 QA 没有稳定单一 class logit，verbalized confidence 又是自报；Sem-ECE 对采样答案做语义分组，用频率估计 confidence，并分开 same-sample 与 held-out estimator 的偏差。语义聚类、采样数与低 margin 会改变校准结果，分数不是 truth 或 release authority；当前 Evaluation 主线已覆盖 estimator identity、sampling variance、calibration 与 evidence boundary。

Method：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.08432v1 §4–§5（semantic classes、Sem1 same-sample、Sem2 held-out estimator、bias analysis）`。

Evaluation：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.08432v1 §6；Appendix C–F（convergence、margin regimes、cross-benchmark calibration、bootstrap）`。

Non-proof / fallback：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.08432v1 §7；Appendix A/E（semantic grouping、sampling budget、low-margin assumptions and bootstrap dependence）`。离开披露条件时回退既有机制，不把作者结果外推为通用保证。

Artifact：`https://github.com/ZhanliangAaronWang/Sem-ECE；exact-v1 未冻结 commit`。

### A Single Layer to Explain Them All:Understanding Massive Activations in Large Language Models

问题与演进：把 massive activation 只归因于 attention sink 会忽略层内生成路径；论文定位单个 emergence layer，并用 RMSNorm 与 FFN 参数、方向刚性和 weight-guided masking 做干预。定位与受测性能变化不证明该层独自拥有所有功能，mask 还会改变 representation；结果只覆盖披露模型与任务。当前 Transformer Layer 主线已承载 residual、norm、FFN/attention 相互作用和 intervention 非充分性。

Method：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.08504v1 §3–§4（ME layer、RMSNorm/FFN amplification、directional rigidity、weight-guided dimension masking）`。

Evaluation：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.08504v1 §5；Appendix B–H（models、mask ablations、layer stability and universality checks）`。

Non-proof / fallback：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.08504v1 Appendix A（tested architectures/tasks、mechanistic attribution and intervention scope）`。离开披露条件时回退既有机制，不把作者结果外推为通用保证。

Artifact：`https://github.com/vanpe20/A-Single-Layer-to-Explain-Them-All-Understanding-Massive-Values-in-Large-Language-Models.git；exact-v1 未冻结 commit`。
<!-- claim:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-08504:start -->长期结论只限 arXiv:2605.08504v1 披露的方法、实验与限制；未披露 model、hardware、precision、length、batch、concurrency、evaluator 或 SLO 记为 Not Disclosed。<!-- claim:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-08504:end -->

### Scaling Limits of Long-Context Transformers

问题与演进：仅用 context length 描述 attention 难度会漏掉 score temperature 与近邻距离尾部；论文在 fixed-query、i.i.d. random context 下推导 supercritical、critical、subcritical 三种权重和输出极限。定理受分布与渐近假设约束，数值实验不证明真实语料、训练后多头网络或系统吞吐；当前 Long Context 主线已覆盖选择性、位置/注意力与长序列容量边界。

Non-proof / fallback：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.16354v1 Limitations: pilot R² sensitivity and corpus representativeness; exact-v1 §6 / Appendix non-proof boundary check — no generalization beyond the disclosed model, workload, hardware, precision, evaluator and SLO`。离开披露条件时回退既有机制，不把作者结果外推为通用保证。Artifact：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.16354v1 — artifact/code statement; immutable event-time commit Not Disclosed unless explicitly named`。

<!-- review:SF-2026-ARXIV-2606-20582:start -->
#### Communication-Semantic-Aware RDMA Loss Recovery for QP-scalable Hyperscale AI Training

问题与演进：`Communication-Semantic-Aware RDMA Loss Recovery for QP-scalable Hyperscale AI Training` 通过“To address these challenges, we propose Communication-Semantic-Aware Unreliable Datagram (CSA-UD), a novel RDMA loss recovery mechanism that combines scalability and reliability.”改变 train distributed training 的可观察机制或决策边界；exact-v1 的证明范围限于“Testbed experiments and ns-3 simulations show that CSA-UD significantly reduces tail latency under large-scale collective communication.”，不能外推到未披露 workload、model、hardware、precision、evaluator 或 SLO。旧方案在其原 workload、风险和成本约束下仍成立。

Method：`https://arxiv.org/html/2606.20582v1 §III CSA-UD adaptive NACK and bitmap reassembly — mechanism: To address these challenges, we propose Communication-Semantic-Aware Unreliable Datagram (CSA-UD), a novel RDMA loss recovery mechanism that combines scalability and reliability.`。

Evaluation：`https://arxiv.org/html/2606.20582v1 §IV testbed/ns-3 collective evaluation — disclosed scope: Current artificial intelligence (AI) infrastructures widely adopt Remote Direct Memory Access (RDMA) to support high-performance communication. Training trillion-parameter models involves frequent collective communication operations, such as All-Reduce and All-to-All, which generate intensive RDMA traffic. Existing RDMA deployments predominantly use the reliable connection (RC) model, where each…`。

Non-proof / fallback：`https://arxiv.org/html/2606.20582v1 Discussion boundary; RDMA fabric/load/topology and simulation scope; exact-v1 §6 / Appendix non-proof boundary check — no generalization beyond the disclosed model, workload, hardware, precision, evaluator and SLO`。离开披露条件时回退既有机制，不把作者结果外推为通用保证。Artifact：`https://arxiv.org/html/2606.20582v1 — artifact/code statement; immutable event-time commit Not Disclosed unless explicitly named`。

<!-- review:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-30359:start -->
#### Kernel Foundry: A Diagnosis-driven Evolutionary Kernel Optimizer with Multi-Experts

问题与演进：`Kernel Foundry: A Diagnosis-driven Evolutionary Kernel Optimizer with Multi-Experts` 通过“We propose Kernel Foundry, a diagnosis-driven evolutionary framework for automatic GPU kernel optimization.”改变 infer tensorrt llm 的可观察机制或决策边界；exact-v1 的证明范围限于“While large language models (LLMs) show promise in code generation, they often fail to produce kernels that are both correct and efficient.”，不能外推到未披露 workload、model、hardware、precision、evaluator 或 SLO。旧方案在其原 workload、风险和成本约束下仍成立。

Method：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.30359v1 §3 diagnosis-driven multi-island kernel evolution — mechanism: While large language models (LLMs) show promise in code generation, they often fail to produce kernels that are both correct and efficient.`。

Evaluation：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.30359v1 §4 KernelBench evaluation — disclosed scope: Generating high-performance GPU kernels remains challenging due to the need for both correctness and hardware-aware optimization. While large language models (LLMs) show promise in code generation, they often fail to produce kernels that are both correct and efficient. We propose Kernel Foundry, a diagnosis-driven evolutionary framework…`。

Non-proof / fallback：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.30359v1 §3.5 limitations; iterative cost, initialization and noisy signals; exact-v1 §6 / Appendix non-proof boundary check — no generalization beyond the disclosed model, workload, hardware, precision, evaluator and SLO`。离开披露条件时回退既有机制，不把作者结果外推为通用保证。Artifact：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.30359v1 — artifact/code statement; immutable event-time commit Not Disclosed unless explicitly named`。

<!-- review:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-30359:end -->

<!-- review:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-08363:start -->
### Kettle: Attested builds for verifiable software provenance

问题与演进：可复现构建以 bitwise rebuild 换高验证成本；Kettle 改为在 CVM 中绑定输入 Merkle root、测量环境、SLSA provenance digest、request nonce 与输出 digest，使 verifier 不必信任 host 或重跑构建。它把信任面缩到 TEE vendor/firmware、密码原语与 source identity，却不证明源码意图、依赖无后门或抵抗侵入式物理攻击。当前 Security 主线已完整覆盖 SLSA、digest、attestation 与 trust-surface 边界。

Method：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.08363v1 §5–§6（input Merkle tree、CVM measured boot、SLSA provenance、nonce-bound attestation、local verification）`。

Evaluation：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.08363v1 §5.1–§5.6、§7.1；architecture/evidence-chain walk-through，无独立 performance benchmark`。

Non-proof / fallback：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.08363v1 §4、§7.2（TEE vendor/firmware and source identity trusted；invasive physical attack、source intent、malicious toolchain boundary）`。离开披露条件时回退既有机制，不把作者结果外推为通用保证。

Artifact：`https://github.com/lunal-dev/kettle；exact-v1 未冻结 commit`。
<!-- claim:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-08363:start -->长期结论只限 arXiv:2605.08363v1 披露的方法、实验与限制；未披露 model、hardware、precision、length、batch、concurrency、evaluator 或 SLO 记为 Not Disclosed。<!-- claim:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-08363:end -->
<!-- review:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-08363:end -->

<!-- review:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-08565:start -->
### Finer is Better (with the Right Scaling)

问题与演进：更小 quantization block 理论上应降低误差，但低精度 scale 可在极小值处下溢为零，FP4 的 4到6 间距又会使细粒度块反而更差；prevent-zero 与 4-over-6 分别修复两个区间，brute force 给出误差上界。结果没有证明端到端 kernel、latency 或所有层质量，且 Ch49 已要求 scale semantics、calibration、layout、kernel 和硬件共同进入 execution plan。

Method：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.08565v1 §II（E4M3 scale、FP4 E2M1、prevent-zero、4-over-6、hierarchical and brute-force scaling）`。

Evaluation：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.08565v1 §III（normal-distribution MSE；Granite/Llama/DeepSeek/Qwen weight-and-MLP-activation perplexity）`。

Non-proof / fallback：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.08565v1 §IV；只验证披露 FP4/scale formats、4 models 与 perplexity，未给出 kernel latency/energy 或全算子覆盖`。离开披露条件时回退既有机制，不把作者结果外推为通用保证。

Artifact：`https://github.com/clee1994/finer_is_better；exact-v1 未冻结 commit`。
<!-- claim:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-08565:start -->长期结论只限 arXiv:2605.08565v1 披露的方法、实验与限制；未披露 model、hardware、precision、length、batch、concurrency、evaluator 或 SLO 记为 Not Disclosed。<!-- claim:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-08565:end -->
<!-- review:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-08565:end -->

<!-- review:SF-2026-ARXIV-2606-27379:start -->
### Position: The Term "Machine Unlearning" Is Overused in LLMs

问题与演进：把模型拒答或目标字符串概率下降称为 unlearning，容易把输出 policy 变化误写成训练数据影响已删除；论文要求把候选模型与不含 forget set 的 provenance-bearing retrain reference 比较，并把 derived-capability recovery probes 纳入评测。Reference 不总是可负担或唯一，等价距离也依赖 threat model；因此可退化为明确标注的 suppression/editing 目标，不能伪称删除保证。当前 Ch66 尚无这条 unlearning-specific evaluation contract，形成可写回增量。

Method：`https://arxiv.org/html/2606.27379v1 §2、§4–§5（dataset-defined deletion guarantee、reference retraining、derived-capability probes）`。

Evaluation：`https://arxiv.org/html/2606.27379v1 §3（TOFU、MUSE、RWKU、WMDP metric analysis and adversarial recovery evidence）；position paper未实现新 unlearning system`。

Non-proof / fallback：`https://arxiv.org/html/2606.27379v1 §6；Appendix A–B（reference provenance/feasibility、policy removal versus deletion、multimodal extension）`。离开披露条件时回退既有机制，不把作者结果外推为通用保证。

Artifact：`exact-v1 为 position/evaluation-contract paper，未披露代码或新 benchmark artifact`。
<!-- claim:SF-2026-ARXIV-2606-27379:start -->长期结论只限 arXiv:2606.27379v1 披露的方法、实验与限制；未披露 model、hardware、precision、length、batch、concurrency、evaluator 或 SLO 记为 Not Disclosed。<!-- claim:SF-2026-ARXIV-2606-27379:end -->
<!-- review:SF-2026-ARXIV-2606-27379:end -->

### Future State：调度当前 stage 时承诺下游状态

<!-- analysis:DA-FUTURE-STATE-SCHEDULING:start -->传统 DAG scheduler 只优化当前 ready stage，在依赖短、模型常驻且无可复用状态时合理；异构 LLM workflow 会把 model residency、parent output locality、prefix reuse 与 device reachability 留给下游。FATE 以 bounded frontier 重复规划，并把当前 placement 诱导出的未来状态计入 cost。它以规划开销和 cost-model 假设换取端到端 latency；模型失配或 DAG 很小时仍应回退简单 heuristic。<!-- analysis:DA-FUTURE-STATE-SCHEDULING:end -->

### KV 压缩：Eviction 与 Quantization 必须共同分配误差预算

<!-- analysis:DA-KV-RATE-DISTORTION:start -->只做 eviction 或只做 quantization 在各自预算固定时简单，却无法回答 token/channel 间该保留数量还是比特数。RDKV 将注意力可见 distortion 校准成统一预算并用组合优化联合分配。收益是同一 memory budget 下更好的质量，但 allocation 在 prefill 后冻结，decode attention shift 会使决策过时；短上下文或 decode 很长时仍应回退动态策略。<!-- analysis:DA-KV-RATE-DISTORTION:end -->

### Trajectory Compaction：压缩 proposal 必须由原轨迹的后续行为验证

<!-- analysis:DA-TRAJECTORY-COMPACTION:start -->同步 compaction 在关键路径生成摘要，旧方案简单但 compactor 不知道未来真正需要的信息。Slipstream 让 agent 继续在原 context 上执行，同时生成候选摘要，再用独立后续轨迹验证 intent、事实和约束是否保存。它以并行算力与 validator/judge 依赖换取更低延迟和可拒绝的 commit；预算不足或独立信号不可得时必须保留原 context 或回退同步压缩。<!-- analysis:DA-TRAJECTORY-COMPACTION:end -->

<!-- books-review:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-07111:start --><!-- existing:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-07111:start -->Ch30 已将 LoRA/full fine-tuning 作为参数更新范围、容量与成本的连续取舍。<!-- existing:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-07111:end --><!-- delta:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-07111:start -->MoLF 的 gradient-guided optimizer routing 是披露模型与 SQL/Medical QA/counterfactual tasks 下的动态选择案例；未建立新的 state/control owner 或通用最优路由。<!-- delta:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-07111:end -->Decision: `No Change — Existing Coverage`。<!-- books-review:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-07111:end -->

<!-- books-review:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-07134:start --><!-- existing:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-07134:start -->Ch75 已把 observation granularity、context budget 与可用证据组织为同一 context contract。<!-- existing:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-07134:end --><!-- delta:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-07134:start -->Region4Web/PageDigest 证明 region-level observation 的一个 WebArena 受限实例，但不改变 context owner、预算失败模式或退回原页面证据的边界。<!-- delta:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-07134:end -->Decision: `No Change — Existing Coverage`。<!-- books-review:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-07134:end -->

<!-- books-review:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-07180:start --><!-- existing:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-07180:start -->Ch84 已覆盖 agent routing、能力目录、评测反馈与 cold-start fallback 的平台责任。<!-- existing:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-07180:end --><!-- delta:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-07180:start -->BoundaryRouter/RouteBench 是从 early experience 学习路由边界的披露案例；它不证明跨任务、跨模型或生产 SLO 下的通用 router ownership。<!-- delta:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-07180:end -->Decision: `No Change — Existing Coverage`。<!-- books-review:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-07180:end -->

<!-- books-review:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-07514:start --><!-- existing:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-07514:start -->Ch26 已要求 embodied/world-action model 同时验证预测一致性、counterfactual action effect 与 policy usefulness。<!-- existing:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-07514:end --><!-- delta:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-07514:start -->dynamic-consistency 指标把既有要求实例化为受限 evaluation contract；未证明披露 benchmark 之外的 causal/planning guarantee。<!-- delta:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-07514:end -->Decision: `No Change — Existing Coverage`。<!-- books-review:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-07514:end -->

<!-- books-review:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-07547:start --><!-- existing:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-07547:start -->Ch56 已把 deadline、admission、resource sharing、priority inversion 与 overload fallback 放在 scheduler owner。<!-- existing:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-07547:end --><!-- delta:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-07547:start -->AI-RAN 的 hierarchical controller 是该机制在披露 radio/AI service contract 下的实例；不能外推为异构 fleet 的通用 latency/SLO 保证。<!-- delta:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-07547:end -->Decision: `No Change — Existing Coverage`。<!-- books-review:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-07547:end -->

<!-- books-review:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-08268:start --><!-- existing:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-08268:start -->Ch82 已覆盖多 agent consensus 中 identity、message provenance、Byzantine/insider threat 与 quorum failure。<!-- existing:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-08268:end --><!-- delta:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-08268:start -->论文的 insider propagation 实验是披露拓扑与协议下的反例；不证明所有 coordination protocol 的攻击率或防御充分性。<!-- delta:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-08268:end -->Decision: `No Change — Existing Coverage`。<!-- books-review:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-08268:end -->

<!-- books-review:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-08363:start --><!-- existing:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-08363:start -->Ch72 已覆盖 SLSA provenance、digest、attestation、TEE measurement 与 verifier trust surface。<!-- existing:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-08363:end --><!-- delta:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-08363:start -->Kettle 把这些机制组合为 nonce-bound CVM build evidence；它仍信任 TEE vendor/firmware 和 source identity，且不证明源码意图或恶意 toolchain 缺失。<!-- delta:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-08363:end -->Decision: `No Change — Existing Coverage`。<!-- books-review:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-08363:end -->

<!-- books-review:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-08368:start --><!-- existing:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-08368:start -->Ch31 已区分 post-training 的 policy elicitation、能力改变、reward signal 与 evaluation owner。<!-- existing:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-08368:end --><!-- delta:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-08368:start -->free-energy formalism 提供区分 elicitation 与 creation 的解释框架；exact-v1 不提供足以替换既有训练/evaluation control chain 的通用实证。<!-- delta:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-08368:end -->Decision: `No Change — Existing Coverage`。<!-- books-review:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-08368:end -->

<!-- books-review:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-08399:start --><!-- existing:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-08399:start -->Ch81 已覆盖 DAG/workflow state、tool execution、credit assignment、evolution 与 checkpoint fallback。<!-- existing:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-08399:end --><!-- delta:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-08399:start -->CoCoDA 是 compositional DAG co-evolution 的受限实现；不能把披露 benchmark 的增益外推成一般 workflow self-improvement guarantee。<!-- delta:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-08399:end -->Decision: `No Change — Existing Coverage`。<!-- books-review:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-08399:end -->

<!-- books-review:SF-2026-ARXIV-2606-27379:start --><!-- existing:SF-2026-ARXIV-2606-27379:start -->Ch66 已覆盖 benchmark provenance、reference artifact、counterfactual probes 与 claim boundary，但尚未区分 unlearning 的 dataset-defined deletion guarantee 与单纯 output suppression。<!-- existing:SF-2026-ARXIV-2606-27379:end --><!-- delta:SF-2026-ARXIV-2606-27379:start -->exact-v1 要求候选模型对齐不含 forget set 的 provenance-bearing retrain reference，并加入 derived-capability recovery probes；reference 不可负担或不唯一时必须明确降级为 suppression/editing 目标，不能宣称删除保证。论文是 position/evaluation-contract work，未实现新 unlearning system，也未提供可外推的删除成功率。<!-- delta:SF-2026-ARXIV-2606-27379:end -->Decision: `Integrate`；status=`post_write_semantic_audit_passed`。<!-- books-review:SF-2026-ARXIV-2606-27379:end -->
